Despite decades of research, existing approaches predominantly optimize hand-crafted proxy objectives, such as estimated wirelength, and typically produce placements through one-shot numerical optimization, limiting their ability to incorporate visual layout context, codified design expertise, and downstream physical-design feedback in a unified loop. We present PlaceReasoner-Beta, a verifier-guided multi-agent framework that reformulates macro placement as a closed-loop reasoning problem rather than black-box optimization. A vision-language model (VLM) planner generates candidate placements from the floorplan image, macro specifications, and connectivity structure; a geometric verifier enforces physical legality and expert placement principles; a physical verifier refines candidates using early implementation feedback; and a post-route optimizer further improves promising layouts using final PPA. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce PlaceReasoner-Bench, a fully open end-to-end benchmark built from open RTL designs, EDA tools, and technology libraries. It comprises 8 designs at two aspect ratios, yielding 16 tasks with fixed floorplans and I/O assignments, so methods differ only in macro positions and orientations and are evaluated using routed PPA and DRC rather than pre-route proxies. Across the benchmark, PlaceReasoner-Beta achieves the best timing among DRC-clean methods on all square tasks, reducing post-route TNS by 61.2% at 1:1 and 53.0% at 2:1 relative to the classical baseline field. It also shortens routed wirelength on most designs despite never explicitly optimizing it, demonstrating that reasoning over spatial structure under physical-design feedback can improve end-to-end layout quality beyond proxy-objective optimization.

</details>


### [178] [Efficient Benchmarking in Production: A Study of an Evolving LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21267)
*Yining She,Lei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对生产级分析型LLM智能体的重复评测成本问题，比较随机抽样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集与IRT自适应测试，发现多维2PL自适应测试分数保真度最佳，但实际部署选择难度分层固定子集以兼顾操作简便性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中LLM智能体随迭代需要反复评测，但完整基准运行成本高昂，因此需要研究高效的重复性评测方法，并分享一线部署经验。

Method: 使用574次生产基准的历史运行记录，按时间顺序划分为校准期与留出期，对比四种方法：随机抽样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集、基于IRT的自适应测试。

Result: 多维2PL自适应测试总体分数保真度最佳，仅执行200题（全量38.5%）即达到1.03个百分点的MAE；难度分层固定子集虽略逊，但无需重新校准即可迁移到其他五个智能体家族，且在短至一天的校准窗口下依然稳定。

Conclusion: 尽管自适应测试精度最高，出于操作简便性最终部署难度分层固定子集，并据此提出面向生产智能体重复评测的实践建议。

Abstract: Production LLM agents are evaluated repeatedly as they evolve, but full agent benchmarks are costly to rerun. We study efficient recurring evaluation for a production analytics agent serving tens of thousands of monthly active users and report first-hand deployment experience. Using 574 historical runs of the production benchmark, split chronologically into calibration and held-out periods, we compare random sampling, historical caching, fixed representative subsets, and IRT-based adaptive testing. The results show that multidimensional 2PL adaptive testing achieves the best overall score fidelity: executing 200 questions, 38.5% of a full run, yields 1.03 pp of MAE. We nevertheless deployed difficulty-stratified fixed subsets because of their operational simplicity, and show they transfer without recalibration to five other agent families and remain stable across calibration windows as short as one day. Drawing on this deployment experience, we report practical recommendations for recurring production-agent evaluation.

</details>


### [179] [GameASG-Bench: Benchmarking Autonomous Software Generation for Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21293)
*Xiuhui Zhang,Yi Chen,Shusheng Xu,Fan Li,Huan Wang,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GameASG-Bench，将行为可测试性纳入游戏开发生成任务，通过静态 L1 与浏览器执行 L2 检查评估智能体生成结果。实验显示最高平均 L2 通过率达 93.2%，但严格任务成功率仅 55.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件生成虽能将需求转为可执行应用，但应用可运行并不代表其交互组件满足指定的行为需求，行为合规性缺乏系统评估手段。

Method: 在生成前声明评估接口规范（固定合法起始场景、玩家级动作、稳定快照、拒绝行为与不变量，保留私有实现开放），构建 47 个浏览器原生游戏生成任务，覆盖 12 类主要游戏类型及 2D/3D 交互，并配套可执行检查与独立验证的参考实现；采用静态 L1 源码级检查与浏览器执行的 L2 语义观察加真实输入和运行时证据检查。

Result: 在九种智能体栈中，最高平均 L2 检查通过率为 93.2%，但最高严格任务成功率仅 55.3%（26/47）。对 DeepSeek-V4-Flash，完整工具访问与更大名义轮次预算带来更多严格任务成功，而严格成功率对推理努力并非单调。两种测试框架均获得 18 个严格任务成功，但仅 10 个任务在两者下均成功。

Conclusion: 高平均检查通过率掩盖了任务级的合规差距，行为可测试性应成为自主软件生成评估的核心组成部分。

Abstract: Autonomous software generation (ASG) aims to turn human requirements into executable applications, but delivering these applications does not necessarily establish that their interacting components satisfy the specified behavioral requirements. We introduce GameASG-Bench, a benchmark that makes behavioral testability part of the generation task for game development. Our design declares an evaluation interface specification before generation, fixing legal starting scenarios, player-level actions, stable snapshots, rejection behavior, and invariants while leaving private implementations open. Concretely, we include: (i) static L1 checks that assess source-level compliance; and (ii) browser-executed L2 checks that combine semantic observations with real input and runtime evidence. We implement this protocol as 47 browser-native game-generation tasks spanning 12 primary genres and both 2D and 3D interaction, each with executable checks and an independently verified reference implementation. Our experiments answer four key questions about end-to-end agent performance, tool access and nominal turn budget, reasoning effort, and harness choice. Across nine agent stacks, the highest observed mean L2 check pass rate is 93.2%, yet the highest observed strict task success rate, requiring all L1 and applicable L2 prerequisite and core requirement checks, is only 55.3% (26/47 tasks). For DeepSeek-V4-Flash, full tool access and larger nominal turn budgets yield more strict task successes, while the strict task success rate is not monotonic in reasoning effort. Both tested harnesses achieve 18 strict task successes, but only ten tasks succeed under both. These results expose task-level compliance gaps that high average check pass rates actually obscure.

</details>


### [180] [LEGIT: Credentialing Protocol for Trustworthy AI Agent Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21325)
*Steve Drew,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LEGIT凭证协议，通过认证、声誉与市场分配机制，将智能体的性能测量绑定到具体配置、任务领域、评估预算和证据上，帮助买方在AI智能体市场中可靠地选择智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体市场正在兴起，具备不同能力的AI智能体自主为买方完成专业任务。但买方难以判断哪个智能体在其任务上表现最佳：已报告的基准分数难以验证，也难以跨任务、软件和预算进行比较。

Method: LEGIT协议将认证、声誉与市场分配相连接。认证通过签名记录，把每个已解决任务的实测质量与成本绑定到智能体配置、任务领域、评估预算和证据；声誉将过往任务结果记录与同一身份关联，并考虑反馈可靠性。买方与智能体可验证凭证记录，并查看可选的视觉画像。此外，在给定女巫攻击模型下，分析操纵声誉所需缴纳的押金与费用。

Result: 评估揭示，观测任务成功率相近的智能体配置之间存在成本差异，且比较结果依赖于评估预算。

Conclusion: 结果支持将性能测量绑定到所测试的配置与资源限制上；补充分析量化了特定女巫攻击模型下操纵声誉所需的押金与费用。

Abstract: Agentic marketplaces are emerging where AI agents with varying capabilities autonomously complete specialized tasks for buyers. A major challenge of such marketplaces is that buyers cannot easily determine which agent will perform best on their tasks. Reported benchmark scores may be difficult to verify or compare across tasks, software, and budgets. We introduce LEGIT, a credentialing protocol connecting certification, reputation, and proposed marketplace allocation. Certification binds measured quality and cost per solved task to an agent configuration, task domain, evaluation budget, and evidence through a signed record. Reputation links records of past task outcomes to the same identity, subject to the reliability of the reported feedback. Buyers and agents can verify credential records and inspect optional visual profiles. Evaluations reveal cost differences between agent configurations with similar observed task success, and show that comparisons depend on the evaluation budget. These results support binding performance measurements to the tested configuration and resource limits. A complementary analysis quantifies the deposits and fees required for reputation manipulation under a stated Sybil attack model.

</details>


### [181] [Offline Multimodal Large Language Models for Decision Support in Air Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21390)
*Joao P. A. Dantas,Jelton A. Cunha,Gabriel Dietzsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将离线大语言模型部署于隔离受限环境作为决策支持工具，提出可离线运行的模块化检索增强架构，并通过对巴西空军四名图像分析员的试点研究，验证系统在教义知识评估中达到与人类相当的水平且耗时更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中作战依赖复杂规则、既定程序，并在连接受限和严格安全约束下进行时间紧迫的分析。分析员需结合书面教义与图像开展工作，且常无法访问外部计算资源。因此有必要探索可在隔离、受限环境中使用、且输出可追溯至原始来源的离线大语言模型决策支持工具。

Method: 提出一种模块化检索增强架构，可在无互联网连接条件下运行，支持来自技术手册的文本与图像输入。开展针对巴西空军四名图像分析员的试点研究，包括：(i) 基于其电子目标识别教义的教义知识评估，比较人类与所提系统在同一测试上的表现；(ii) 测量在无AI辅助下人工生成侦察目标报告（REMIR）所涉及的认知负荷。

Result: 结果显示，人工任务要求较高，尤其在心理需求（6.0/7）和努力程度（5.0/7）方面；所提系统在评估中达到与人类相同分数（8/10），耗时7.1分钟，而人类平均耗时26.5分钟，为未来AI辅助评估建立了基线。

Conclusion: 离线检索增强大语言模型系统可在无网络、受限安全环境下提供可追溯的教义知识支持，并在知识评估任务中匹敌人类分析员且显著缩短时间。作者最后描述了一套未来评估协议，用于系统比较人工与AI辅助工作流。

Abstract: Air operations rely on complex rules, established procedures, and time-critical analysis under limited connectivity and strict security constraints. In such environments, analysts must combine written doctrine with images, often without access to external computing resources. This paper studies offline large language models as decision support tools, deployed in isolated and restricted environments to give analysts access to doctrinal knowledge that remains traceable to its original sources through natural language interaction. We describe a modular retrieval-augmented architecture suitable for operation without Internet connectivity, supporting both text and image input from technical manuals. As a first step toward evaluating this architecture, we report a pilot study with four image analysts of the Brazilian Air Force, combining (i) a doctrinal knowledge assessment based on their electronic-target identification doctrine, comparing human and proposed system performance on the same test, and (ii) a measurement of the cognitive workload involved in manually producing a reconnaissance target report (Relatório de Missão de Reconhecimento - REMIR) without AI assistance. The results show a demanding manual task, especially in terms of mental demand (6.0/7) and effort (5.0/7), while the proposed system matches the human score (8/10) and completes the assessment in 7.1 minutes (compared to a human average of 26.5 minutes), establishing a baseline for future AI-assisted evaluation. Finally, we describe a future evaluation protocol to systematically compare manual and AI-assisted workflows.

</details>


### [182] [DENSE: Distilling Agent Trajectories into Evidence-Grounded Shortcut Trees for Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21423)
*Siyuan Liu,Fan Yu,Dongyu Ru,Yizhu Liu,Yifan Yang,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DENSE 方法，将智能体执行轨迹蒸馏为可复用的证据化嵌套捷径树反馈，并设计 REFIT 评估协议，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上显著提升严格通过率并减少重跑 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线智能体部署会产生大量执行轨迹，但任务特定验证与专家标注难以规模化。作者希望在没有事后结果标签的情况下，从轨迹中的局部进展、恢复过程与未完成需求等证据中蒸馏出可复用的反馈。

Method: 提出 DENSE，将证据组织为基于证据的嵌套子任务捷径树：压缩冗余尝试、利用恢复证据跨层协调问题、对已完成分支进行总结并展开未解决分支，从而把可复用进展与剩余任务关联。同时提出 REFIT 源配对协议，在事后结果盲测下比较来自共享初始轨迹的反馈，并重置环境与模型上下文以进行同任务的全新尝试。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，DENSE 在四个接收模型中取得测试过的非特权反馈方法里最高的严格通过率；相对初始执行，严格通过率提升 7.12–15.64 个百分点，重跑时观察到的接收方 token 减少 19.0–43.6%。GPT-5.5 消融实验支持将嵌套子任务分析与捷径构建、问题协调相结合。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于证据的轨迹复用可推动智能体自我改进，并减少对外部监督的依赖。

Abstract: Online agent deployments produce abundant execution traces, while task-specific verification and expert annotation are costly to scale. We study how to distill these traces into reusable feedback without post-hoc outcome labels, drawing on their evidence of local progress, recovery, and unfinished requirements. We introduce DENSE (Distilling Evidence from Nested Subtask Executions), which organizes this evidence into evidence-grounded nested shortcut trees. DENSE compresses redundant attempts, reconciles issues across levels using recovery evidence, and summarizes completed branches while expanding unresolved ones, linking reusable progress to remaining obligations. We introduce REFIT, a source-paired protocol comparing feedback from shared initial trajectories under post-hoc outcome blindness, with environments and model contexts reset for fresh attempts at the same tasks. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, DENSE achieves the highest strict pass rate among tested non-privileged feedback methods across four recipient models. Relative to initial executions, strict pass rate improves by 7.12-15.64 pp, with 19.0-43.6% fewer observed recipient tokens in reruns. GPT-5.5 ablations support combining nested subtask analysis with shortcut construction and issue reconciliation. These findings point toward agent self-refinement through evidence-grounded trajectory reuse with less reliance on external supervision.

</details>


### [183] [GVPO++: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21432)
*Kaichen Zhang,Yuzhong Hong,Junwei Bao,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dingqian Hong,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GVPO（组方差策略优化），通过将 KL 约束奖励最大化的解析解融入梯度加权，避免重要性采样，从而解决后训练不稳定问题，并自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（如 GRPO）依赖重要性采样，导致实际部署中训练不稳定，限制了应用。

Method: 提出 GVPO，将 KL 约束奖励最大化的解析解整合进梯度加权方案；其梯度对应隐式奖励中心距离与实际奖励中心距离之间的均方误差，支持灵活采样分布且无需重要性采样。

Result: GVPO 具备两项优势：(1) 保证唯一最优解，且精确对应 KL 约束奖励最大化目标；(2) 允许灵活采样分布，无需重要性采样。此外，GVPO 可自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏，并支持优化一族扩展的 OPD 目标。

Conclusion: GVPO 统一了理论保证与实际适应性，为可靠且通用的 LLM 后训练与在线策略蒸馏建立了新范式。

Abstract: Post-training plays a pivotal role in enhancing the reasoning capabilities and task-specific expertise of large language models (LLMs). Despite recent advances in post-training methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), their practical deployment remains impeded by training instability arising from the reliance on importance sampling.
  We introduce Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO), a novel post-training method that integrates the analytical solution of KL-constrained reward maximization into its gradient weighting scheme. This formulation provides an intuitive interpretation: GVPO's gradient corresponds to the mean squared error between the central distance of implicit rewards and that of actual rewards. GVPO offers two key advantages: (1) it guarantees a unique optimal solution, exactly to the KL-constrained reward maximization objective, and (2) it enables flexible sampling distributions without requiring importance sampling.
  Beyond general post-training, we show that GVPO naturally extends to on-policy distillation (OPD). Furthermore, GVPO enables the optimization of a broad family of extended OPD objectives, providing a principled foundation for diverse objective design. By unifying theoretical guarantees with practical adaptability, GVPO establishes a new paradigm for reliable and versatile LLM post-training and on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [184] [Risk-Aware Occupancy for Safety-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21470)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,Yiren Zhao,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知占据（risk-aware occupancy）这一密集表示，替代稀疏表示用于端到端自动驾驶，并设计ROIDrive网络，在nuScenes上实现显著的开环碰撞降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏表示将环境感知建模为离散元素（物体、车道线），在拥挤、遮挡、非结构化障碍、不确定区域和复杂交互场景中存在安全风险。

Method: 提出风险感知占据表示，联合编码全局场景占据、地图导出的交通约束和未来动态智能体占据为统一BEV图，捕捉时空维度的风险证据；设计E2E网络ROIDrive，用独立分支预测风险感知占据并注入规划查询以生成安全导向轨迹；引入基于nuScenes和occ3d-nuScenes的RiskOcc4D-nuScenes量化安全问题。

Result: 在nuScenes上，相对开环碰撞降低52.9%（UniAD指标）和35.0%（ST-P3指标）。

Conclusion: 风险感知占据以显式统一方式表征规划相关风险，有效提升端到端驾驶安全性。

Abstract: Sparse representation formulates the environment perception for the end-to-end driving system as a set of discrete elements like objects and lane lines. This formulation meets safety risks in crowded, occluded scenes dealing with unstructured obstacles, uncertain regions, and intricate interactions. In this paper, we propose a dense representation, risk-aware occupancy, to characterize planning-relevant risks in an explicit and uniform manner. It jointly encodes global scene occupancy, map-derived traffic constraints, and future dynamic agent occupancy into a unified BEV map. The unified BEV map captures the risk evidence for trajectory planning in both spatial and temporal dimensions. We design an E2E network, ROIDrive, to realize risk-aware occupancy. It predicts risk-aware occupancy with an independent branch and injects it into planning queries for safety-oriented trajectory generation. In addition, to quantify the safety problem, we introduce RiskOcc4D-nuScenes built upon nuscenes and occ3d-nuscenes. Our risk-aware occupancy yields relative open-loop collision reductions of 52.9% under the UniAD metric and 35.0% under the ST-P3 metric on nuScenes.

</details>


### [185] [Driving on Registers, Reasoning on Risk: Risk-Aware Occupancy for Register-Based End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21486)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,YuKai Qin,Yiren Zhao,Lidong Yu,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RRDrive，利用风险感知占用表示提升端到端自动驾驶多模态轨迹预测的生成与选择质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶的多模态轨迹预测受限于稀疏场景表示。证据不完整导致候选轨迹生成质量低，且几何相似轨迹间的排序不可靠：在register基线上，差/较差候选占比19.74%，而oracle最优候选平均仅排第33.9位。

Method: RRDrive引入风险感知占用（risk-aware occupancy）作为稠密、时间对齐、可由轨迹查询的表示。其全局结构指导高质量多模态生成，候选条件化风险查询支持细粒度选择。同时构建带自动风险标注的RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM数据集；为进一步脱离oracle设定，开发从传感器输入直接估计风险感知占用的外部RiskOcc预测器。

Result: RRDrive取得选中轨迹PDMS 0.951，相对基线0.937提升1.5%；平均候选PDMS提升7.7%。在困难场景中候选PDMS提升30.2%，好候选间的Spearman相关性由0.26提升至0.67（+0.41）。外部RiskOcc预测器也展现出有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 风险感知占用是一种稠密且可查询的有效表示，能同时改善多模态轨迹的生成质量与选择排序，且可由外部预测器从传感器输入估计，验证了该表示的可行性与实用潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal trajectory prediction improves behavioral coverage in end-to-end autonomous driving, but existing methods remain limited by sparse scene representations. Incomplete evidence leads to low-quality candidate generation and unreliable ranking among geometrically similar trajectories. On a register-based baseline, bad and poor candidates constitute 19.74% of the candidate set, while the oracle-best candidate ranks only 33.9th on average. We propose RRDrive, which introduces risk-aware occupancy as a dense, temporally aligned, and trajectory-queryable representation. Its global structure guides high-quality multimodal generation, while candidate-conditioned risk queries support fine-grained selection. We further construct RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM with automatic risk annotations. RRDrive achieves a selected-trajectory PDMS of 0.951, representing a 1.5% relative improvement over the baseline (0.937), and improves the average candidate PDMS by 7.7%. In challenging scenes, it improves candidate PDMS by 30.2% and increases the Spearman correlation among good candidates by 0.41, from 0.26 to 0.67. To move beyond this oracle setting, we further develop an external RiskOcc predictor, a perception module that estimates risk-aware occupancy directly from sensor inputs. The competitive performance validates the representation's feasibility.

</details>


### [186] [LogicTrack: Auditing Reasoning Trajectories of Large Language Models with Formal Logic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21492)
*Jingyu Hu,Shu Yang,Weiru Liu,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LogicTrack 是一个神经符号框架，通过将推理步骤自动形式化为符号表示并用自动定理证明器验证，借助求解器回溯奖励（SBR）在推理时指导回溯树搜索，并构造带回溯轨迹的 SFT 数据，从而提升 CoT 推理链的可验证性与最终答案通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 优化方法多依赖基于结果的反馈，忽视了中间推理步骤的逻辑有效性，导致 LLM 可能通过逻辑上有缺陷的中间链条得到正确最终答案。该研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 LogicTrack 神经符号框架：将每个推理步骤自动形式化为符号表示，用自动定理证明器进行验证；引入 Solver-Based Backtracking Reward（SBR）逐步打分机制，量化逻辑合理性并在推理时引导回溯树搜索；进一步扩展该方法生成带回溯轨迹的监督微调（SFT）数据，使微调模型内化逐步审计能力。

Result: 在 8 个推理基准和 7 个 LLM 上的大量实验表明，LogicTrack 有效提升了推理链的可验证性和最终答案通过率，从而增强了高风险领域中 CoT 的整体质量与可信度。

Conclusion: LogicTrack 通过神经符号验证与步骤级奖励机制，弥补了结果导向反馈对中间逻辑验证不足的问题，并可转化为模型的内在能力，提升 CoT 推理的可靠性与可信度。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has been shown to improve the performance of large language models (LLMs), yet existing optimization methods largely rely on outcome-based feedback, leaving the logical validity of intermediate reasoning steps largely unverified. To address the gap whereby LLMs arrive at correct final answers through logically flawed intermediate reasoning chains, we propose LogicTrack, a neuro-symbolic framework that audits reasoning trajectories by auto-formalizing each reasoning step into symbolic representations and verifying it with automated theorem provers. LogicTrack introduces Solver-Based Backtracking Reward (SBR), a step-wise scoring mechanism that quantifies logical soundness and guides backtracking tree search at inference time. We further extend LogicTrack to construct supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data with backtracking traces from its trajectories, enabling fine-tuned models to internalize step-wise auditing as an intrinsic capability. Extensive experiments across 8 reasoning benchmarks and 7 LLMs demonstrate that LogicTrack effectively improves both the verifiability of reasoning chains and final answer pass rate, thereby enhancing overall CoT quality and trustworthiness in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [187] [PolyBridgeBench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs for Physics-Grounded Bridge Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21493)
*Zicheng Zhao,Dongyin Chen,Rui Xu,Yinghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PolyBridgeBench，一个可执行的多模态桥梁设计基准，用于评估 MLLM 能否综合完整的承重结构并在仿真失败后进行修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型评测多关注空间推理、结构有效性或物理构建，但无法判断模型是否能生成完整承重结构，以及在仿真执行暴露失败后进行修复。缺乏端到端的可执行评测手段。

Method: 模型接收视觉场景与结构化工程约束，生成完整的节点—构件—材料拓扑；通过确定性合法性检查门控，在原生动态物理仿真中执行；执行失败后返回失败轨迹的时序视觉证据，并在固定交互预算下评估修复能力。分别测量确定性有效性、动态功能成功率和失败后恢复。

Result: 在189个关卡上测试六种代表性MLLM，发现确定性有效性与动态成功之间存在显著差距，对材料预算高度敏感，且在严格预算主设定下失败后恢复能力有限。

Conclusion: PolyBridgeBench 通过可执行仿真与分阶段测量，揭示了当前MLLM在真实承重结构设计与失败修复上的明显不足，为该方向提供了细粒度评测框架。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models, or MLLMs, perform well at visual understanding and structured generation, yet these capabilities do not establish whether an engineering design will work when executed. Existing benchmarks assess spatial reasoning, structural validity, or physics-grounded construction, but they do not determine whether MLLMs can synthesize complete load-bearing structures and repair them after simulator execution exposes a failure. We introduce PolyBridgeBench, an executable benchmark for multimodal bridge design. A model receives a visual scene and structured engineering constraints and generates a complete node--member--material topology. Deterministic legality checks gate execution in a native dynamic physics simulation. Following an execution failure, the benchmark returns temporal visual evidence from the failed rollout and evaluates repair under a fixed interaction budget. Separate measurements of deterministic validity, dynamic functional success, and post-failure recovery identify the stage at which design fails. Experiments with six representative MLLMs across 189 levels expose a substantial gap between deterministic validity and dynamic success, pronounced sensitivity to material budgets, and limited post-failure recovery under the primary strict-budget setting.

</details>


### [188] [The Communication Bottleneck: A Round-Trip Study of Tree-Structured Expression Serialization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21509)
*Xavier Suau,Alex Ferrando de las Morenas,Luca Zappella,Samy Bengio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"往返协议"来实证测量结构化信息在自然语言序列化中的损失：生成器把算术表达式转成文字应用题，提取器仅凭应用题还原表达式，符号等价作为精确判据。对16个模型的全部两两组合评估得到通信矩阵，发现该信道有损且不对称、失败主要源于生成端且由树结构难度驱动，并且该信道可通过微调显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在链式思考或交换自由文本中间结果时，会把结构化信息序列化为自然语言。作者想探究：这种"自然语言瓶颈"中究竟有多少树结构化的组合内容能够幸存下来，从而定位模型间交流层次结构时的主要限制因素。

Method: 提出往返（round-trip）协议：1）生成器将程序化生成的算术表达式转换为文字应用题；2）独立的提取器仅从应用题还原表达式；3）用符号等价作为精确评判标准。评估16个模型的全部两两组合，构建通信矩阵，其边际量可分离生成质量与提取质量。此外用约3600条共享算子与树形的微调样本进行训练，并设置使用新算子与新词汇的不相交领域实验以验证泛化性。

Result: 三点主要发现：一、信道有损且不对称，交换生成与提取模型可使准确率变化高达60.4个百分点，最佳配对（两端用不同模型）达92.9%，优于同一模型配对。二、至少73.6%的往返失败源于生成端，难度由树结构（算子数量、深度、右分支）驱动，而非模型家族。三、信道可训练：约3600条微调样本使所有开放权重模型超过未训练的Gemini-3.1-Pro；使用新算子与词汇的不相交领域实验同样提升所有开放权重模型，证明增益并非匹配语义的假象，但与前沿仍存在差距。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同表明，当模型通过自然语言交流层次化结构时，树结构表达式的序列化是主要限制因素。

Abstract: When language models reason in chain-of-thought or exchange free-text intermediates, they serialize structured information into natural language. How much tree-structured compositional content survives this bottleneck? We propose a round-trip protocol that answers this question empirically for tree-structured expressions. A generator converts a procedurally generated arithmetic expression into a word problem, a separate extractor recovers the expression from the word problem alone, and symbolic equivalence provides an exact oracle. Evaluating all pairwise combinations of sixteen models yields a communication matrix whose marginals separate generation quality from extraction quality. Three main findings emerge. First, the channel is lossy and asymmetric: swapping which model generates and which extracts shifts accuracy by up to 60.4 points, and the best pair reaches 92.9% by combining different models on each end rather than the same model on both. Second, at least 73.6% of round-trip failures originate at generation, and difficulty is driven by tree structure (operator count, depth, right-branching) rather than model family. Third, the channel is trainable: ~3600 fine-tuning examples that share the evaluation's operators and tree shapes lift every open-weight model above untrained Gemini-3.1-Pro, an upper bound under matched semantics. A disjoint-domain regime with new operators and vocabulary also raises every open-weight model, confirming the gain is not an artifact of matched semantics, though a gap to the frontier remains. Together these results identify tree-structured expression serialization as a primary limiting factor when models communicate hierarchical structure through natural language.

</details>


### [189] [Learning-to-Optimize as the Missing Architectural Layer of AI-Native Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21519)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Pierpaolo Salvo,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"学习优化（L2O）"作为AI原生通信网络中连接优化与AI智能之间缺失的架构层，把优化算法重新定义为离线知识生成器，为神经代理模型提供监督信息，并给出四阶段工作流与NR-V2X中继选择案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正成为未来AI原生通信网络的基础设计原则，可支撑自主资源管理、自适应控制与零接触运维。但现有AI原生架构虽在网络功能中嵌入智能，却缺乏关于"优化知识应如何被系统性地生成、迁移与利用"的指导，即缺少一个衔接优化与AI智能的架构层。

Method: 提出以学习优化（L2O）作为架构抽象层：不再将优化仅视为在线决策引擎，而是把优化算法重新定义为离线知识生成器，为神经代理模型产生高质量监督信息。给出通用四阶段L2O工作流：优化、知识生成、代理学习、运行时推理。与传统侧重算法加速的L2O不同，该框架适用于异构通信与计算系统。以NR-V2X中继选择问题为例，用MILP求解器生成的优化解训练图神经网络，实现近最优实时决策。

Result: 在NR-V2X中继选择案例中，由MILP求解器生成的优化解成功训练出图神经网络，该网络能够在运行时实时复现近最优决策，验证了四阶段L2O工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: 学习优化（L2O）被定位为未来AI原生网络的关键架构使能者，为优化知识与AI智能之间提供了系统性的衔接范式。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is becoming a fundamental design principle of future AI-native communication networks, enabling autonomous resource management, adaptive control, and zero-touch network operation. While current AI-native architectures increasingly embed intelligence across network functions, they provide little guidance on how optimisation knowledge should be systematically generated, transferred, and exploited by AI models. This paper argues that the Learning-to-Optimize (L2O) represents the missing architectural layer between optimisation and AI-native intelligence. Rather than viewing optimisation merely as an online decision engine, the proposed paradigm redefines optimisation algorithms as offline knowledge generators that produce high-quality supervisory information for neural surrogate models. The resulting models inherit optimisation expertise while enabling low-latency runtime inference suitable for dynamic network environments. A generic four-stage L2O workflow is introduced, comprising optimisation, knowledge generation, surrogate learning, and runtime inference. Unlike existing Learning-to-Optimize approaches, which primarily focus on algorithm acceleration, the proposed framework establishes L2O as an architectural abstraction applicable across heterogeneous communication and computing systems. The proposed paradigm is illustrated by an NR-V2X relay-selection problem, in which optimisation-generated solutions from a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solver are used to train a Graph Neural Network that can reproduce near-optimal decisions in real time. The presented perspective positions Learning-to-Optimize as a key architectural enabler for future AI-native networks.

</details>


### [190] [Dual-Interest Sequential Product Recommendation With Multi-Granular SSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21548)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSRec，一种双兴趣交叉SSM模型，通过解耦长短期语义上下文中的物品角色来提升序列推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的序列推荐受限于成本效率；而现有SSM方法多用单一静态上下文角色编码物品，忽视了物品多义性（同一物品在不同用户上下文中扮演不同语义角色），也难以捕捉不同时间粒度下的动态行为。

Method: DSRec包含长短期两个兴趣分支：长期兴趣嵌入通过历史聚合捕捉稳定偏好，短期兴趣分支由点击时间间隔调制以强调局部会话意图；分别用全序列Mamba做长期建模、用时间调制SSM根据时间间隔动态调整状态演化；并采用残差交叉融合机制在两个分支间交换上下文信息，同时保持语义独立性。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上的实验表明，DSRec优于其他最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦物品角色并结合双分支SSM与跨粒度对齐，DSRec在序列推荐任务上取得更优性能。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation aims to predict the next item a user will interact with based on their historical behavior. Advances in Transformers have significantly improved sequential recommendation but are still limited by cost efficiency. Although State Space Models (SSMs) have recently enabled efficient long-range modeling, most existing methods encode each item with a single static contextual role, overlooking the phenomenon of item polysemy. In fact, the same item often plays different semantic roles depending on user context, and existing methods are limited in capturing dynamic behavior across different temporal granularities. In this work, we propose DSRec, a novel dual-interest cross-SSM model that explicitly disentangles item roles across long-term and short-term semantic context. Sequential items are encoded into long-term interest embeddings that capture stable preferences via historical aggregation, and a short-term interest branch that emphasizes local session intent modulated by inter-click time intervals. These interest embeddings are processed through distinct SSM encoders: a full-sequence Mamba for long-term modeling, and a time-modulated SSM that dynamically adjusts state evolution based on temporal gaps. To enable effective cross-granularity alignment, we adopt a residual cross-fusion mechanism that exchanges contextual information between the two branches while preserving semantic independence. Experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that DSRec outperforms other state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [191] [Beyond Accuracy: Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss for Structured Fraudulent Job Posting Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21599)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Malaika Raza,Muhammad Shoaib Siddiqui,Muhammad Rafi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出质心引导对比损失（CGCL），将分类与密集聚类统一，通过质心驱动的top-k推拉机制重塑潜在空间，用于欺诈招聘广告检测，并在EMSCAD上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈招聘广告检测对求职者与雇主生态有重要影响。现有研究缺乏能同时实现高准确率、并构建具有意义且能刻画虚假帖子细微差异的潜在空间表示的方法。

Method: 提出Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss（CGCL），一种将分类与密集聚类统一的损失函数，通过质心驱动的top-k推拉机制持续重塑潜在空间，从而同时强化准确决策边界与高聚类紧凑性。

Result: 在公开基准数据集EMSCAD上的大量实验表明，该方法达到了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: CGCL的互补特性使模型能兼顾类别可分性与潜在结构，为欺诈招聘广告检测提供了有效方案；代码已开源。

Abstract: Fraudulent job posting detection aims to identify job advertisements that are corrupted either through fake content, misleading information, or negative intent, disrupting the online eco-system of job-seekers and employers. Existing studies in this domain lack effective methods to simultaneously achieve high accuracy and meaningful structure of latent-space representations that capture subtleties among fake posts. To this end, we propose Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss (CGCL), a loss function which unifies classification with densely formulated clustering to consistently reshape latent-space through a centroid-driven top-$k$ push-and-pull mechanism. The complementary nature of CGCL enables the model to enforce accurate decision boundaries and maintain high clustering compactness, effectively capturing both class separability and latent structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance of our method on EMSCAD, a public benchmark dataset. The code associated with this work is available at: https://github.com/ali-ahmed925/CGCL_code

</details>


### [192] [Reducing Barriers to Academic Support: Evaluating a Course-Specific RAG System for Addressing Help-Seeking Disparities in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21600)
*Andy Gray,Jake Hobbs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估了Beacon——一个课程专属的RAG系统，为学生提供私密、即时、与模块对齐的学术支持，以降低求助门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术支持获取不平等，部分学生因焦虑、怕被评判、期望不明或信心不足而不敢求助；在编程等累进性、高认知负荷的学科尤为突出；通用生成式AI虽被广泛使用，但回答常不准确、缺乏情境化或与模块期望不符。

Method: 采用基于设计的研究方法，基于经批准的教学材料构建课程专属RAG系统Beacon，并进行迭代开发；通过问卷与半结构化访谈的混合方法，对高校学生和教职员工进行评估。

Result: 学生认为Beacon的回答与模块内容高度契合，比不受限的生成式AI工具更可信，并认可其使用伪代码和脚手架式解释而非直接给出答案；参与者对未经核实即信任AI回答持谨慎态度，但将其视为咨询讲师或官方资源前的有价值的第一支持点。

Conclusion: 精心设计的课程专属AI系统可通过占据自主学习和正式支持之间的中间空间来降低学术支持获取障碍；教育AI的价值在于扩大指导的可及性，同时保留讲师的教学角色，而非取代教育者。

Abstract: Access to academic support is a key determinant of student success, yet students experience it unequally: some readily seek help from lecturers or tutors, while others hesitate due to anxiety, fear of judgement, uncertainty about expectations, or low confidence in their understanding. This may be especially evident in computing education, where programming tasks are cumulative and cognitively demanding. Although students increasingly turn to general-purpose generative AI tools, these can produce responses that are inaccurate, insufficiently contextualised, or misaligned with module expectations. This study presents and evaluates Beacon, a course-specific Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system providing private, immediate, module-aligned academic support. Grounding responses in approved teaching materials, Beacon was designed to lower barriers to help-seeking while encouraging independent learning. Using a design-based research approach, Beacon was developed iteratively and evaluated via mixed methods, combining questionnaires and semi-structured interviews with students and staff at a Higher Education institution. Students described Beacon's responses as closely aligned with module content and more trustworthy than unrestricted generative AI tools, valuing its use of pseudocode and scaffolded explanations over direct solutions. Although participants remained cautious about trusting AI-generated responses without verification, they viewed the system as a valuable first point of support before consulting lecturers or official resources. The findings suggest that carefully designed course-specific AI systems may reduce barriers to academic support by occupying an intermediary space between independent study and formal support. Rather than replacing educators, educational AI may be most valuable when it broadens access to guidance while preserving the pedagogical role of lecturers.

</details>


### [193] [Calibrating Teacher--Student Discrepancy for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21619)
*Qiangqiang He,Jin Li,MingCai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出校准式同策略蒸馏（Cal-OPD），通过正负特权干预估计教师自身的偏差区域，仅保留超出该区域的师生差异作为优化信号，在数学推理基准上以约52–65%的信号量稳定超越标准OPD及变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准同策略蒸馏（OPD）让学生学习教师与学生的token级差异，但该差异并非纯粹反映师生能力差距，还混杂了教师自身的偏差；在特权OPD中，特权信息会引发更大的教师侧似然偏移，使学生更多地学到教师自身的偏差，问题被进一步放大。

Method: Cal-OPD通过正向与负向特权干预来估计教师的"自偏差区域"，并对原始师生差异进行校准，只保留落在该区域之外的分量作为优化信号，从而剔除教师自身偏差造成的影响。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，Cal-OPD仅保留原始师生差异的约52–65%作为优化信号，却在不同模型规模上持续优于标准OPD及其变体。

Conclusion: 师生差异不应被无条件地当作学习目标，剔除教师自身偏差后的校准信号更有效，说明蒸馏信号的质量（纯净度）比数量更关键。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves reasoning models by learning the token-level discrepancy between a stronger teacher and an on-policy student. However, this discrepancy does not purely reflect the capability gap between the teacher and the student: it also contains deviations arising from the teacher itself, which are consequently mixed into the observed teacher--student discrepancy and indiscriminately learned by standard OPD during training. This issue is further exacerbated by privileged OPD, where privileged information induces larger teacher-side likelihood shifts, thereby encouraging the student to learn more of the teacher's own deviation. We introduce \textbf{Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (Cal-OPD)}, which estimates the teacher's self-deviation region through positive and negative privileged interventions and calibrates the original teacher--student discrepancy by retaining only the component that lies beyond this region. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that, while retaining only about 52--65\% of the original teacher--student discrepancy as the optimization signal, Cal-OPD consistently outperforms standard OPD and its variants across model scales.

</details>


### [194] [One Prompt Does Not Fit All: Self-Meta-Evolve for Personalized Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21626)
*Hongliang Li,Lu Wang,Yong Xu,Hanyang Chen,Zhitao Hou,Xiaoting Qin,Song Ge,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Meta-Evolve分层框架，面向企业信息抽取中的用户异质性，为每位用户维护并持续优化专属提示词，并发布基于职业画像的基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业信息抽取场景中同一文档需针对不同用户重新组织，但现有提示词优化方法仅依赖单一提示词和全局目标，与真实工作场所中的用户异质性不匹配。

Method: 将企业信息抽取建模为交互反馈下的按用户提示词适配；提出Self-Meta-Evolve双层框架：内层循环根据角色画像条件反馈编辑结构化提示词，外层循环通过蒸馏成功编辑模式演化元提示词；并发布含292个模拟企业用户的画像驱动基准及可复现的画像生成流程。

Result: 在基准上达到74.58%成功率，比最强提示词优化基线高出13.56个百分点，仅两轮迭代即达52.54%；20名真实从业者的双盲人工研究显示，本框架适配的提示词在71%的成对比较中胜过静态基线。

Conclusion: 按用户自适应提示词能有效应对企业信息抽取的用户异质性，双层演化机制与画像基准可提升性能与效率，并获得真实从业者的验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for enterprise information extraction (IE), where the same document must be reorganized differently for each user. Existing prompt optimization methods, however, rely on a single prompt optimized against a global objective, which is misaligned with the inherent user heterogeneity of real workplaces. We formulate enterprise IE as per-user prompt adaptation under interaction feedback and propose Self-Meta-Evolve, a hierarchical framework that maintains a dedicated prompt for each user and continuously refines it through a dual-loop process: an inner loop that edits structured prompts based on persona-conditioned feedback, and an outer loop that evolves the meta-prompt itself by distilling successful editing patterns. To enable scalable training and evaluation, we release a persona-driven IE benchmark of 292 simulated enterprise users, paired with a reproducible persona-generation pipeline grounded in O*NET occupational taxonomies. On this benchmark, Self-Meta-Evolve achieves a 74.58% success rate, outperforming the strongest prompt-optimization baseline by 13.56 absolute points, and reaches 52.54\% within only two iterations. A double-blind human study with twenty real professionals further confirms that prompts adapted by our framework win against static baselines in 71% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [195] [Accelerating Dense LLMs via L0-regularized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21672)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Jiudong Yang,Zhaowen Tao,Meng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出L0-MoE，一种轻量级MoE方法，通过L0正则化加速稠密大语言模型，实现最高2.5倍加速且几乎无性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理慢且成本高；现有加速方法常带来明显性能下降，而传统MoE模型需要大量计算资源。

Method: 采用L0正则化构建轻量级MoE以加速稠密LLM；引入聚类混淆矩阵进行领域感知的数据集构建，并应用动态批处理实现高效训练。

Result: L0-MoE相比稠密模型实现最高2.5倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的性能，优于现有LLM加速基线方法。

Conclusion: L0-MoE可在几乎不损失性能的前提下有效加速稠密大语言模型，为LLM高效推理提供了新的轻量级方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but suffer from slow and costly inference. Existing acceleration methods often lead to noticeable performance degradation, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models require extensive computational resources. In this paper, we propose L0-MoE, a lightweight MoE approach using L0-regularization to accelerate dense LLMs nearly without performance loss. Our method introduces a cluster confusion matrix for domain-aware dataset curation and applies dynamic batching for efficient training. Experiments show that L0-MoE achieves up to 2.5x speedup over dense models while maintaining competitive performance, outperforming existing LLM acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [196] [GUARD: Natural Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models via Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21677)
*Zeyu Yan,Guanghao Zhou,Minghui Qiu,Ming Gao,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GUARD 方法，通过将大推理模型的非安全披露转化为"安全退出轨迹"并进行引导蒸馏，实现更自然的机器遗忘，同时提出 NFRS 指标衡量遗忘后输出的替代质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型（LRM）的机器遗忘存在挑战：受保护事实或不安全推理可能出现在中间思维链（CoT）轨迹中，而非仅在最终答案。现有遗忘目标仅抑制目标内容或重定向内部表示，但未指定遗忘后轨迹应如何继续，导致幻觉替代、边界畸形或重复输出等问题。

Method: 提出 Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD)：将模型生成的不安全披露转化为安全退出轨迹（连贯的非披露 CoT 加稳定的拒绝式回答），通过引导 token 对齐冻结的 LRM，并将引导行为蒸馏到模型参数中。此外提出 Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS) 指标，评估遗忘输出的结构稳定性、流畅性及无依据替代内容。

Result: 在 R-TOFU 和基于 STAR-1 的有害意图设置上的大量实验表明，GUARD 在两个广泛采用的蒸馏 LRM 上大幅减少不安全与隐私披露，同时保留推理效用。

Conclusion: GUARD 通过学习自然的遗忘轨迹（安全退出轨迹）并蒸馏到模型参数，有效提升了大推理模型遗忘的安全性与质量，NFRS 指标补充了超越泄漏率之外的评价维度。代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have made machine unlearning more challenging, as protected facts or unsafe rationales may surface in intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before the final answer is produced. Existing unlearning objectives typically suppress the target content or redirect internal representations, but they never specify how the post-forgetting trajectory should continue, which can lead to hallucinated substitutes, malformed boundaries, or repetitive outputs. We argue that LRM unlearning should instead learn a natural forgetting trajectory: a coherent non-disclosing CoT followed by a stable refusal-style answer that replace the original disclosure. To this end, we propose Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD), which converts model-generated unsafe disclosures into safe-exit trajectories, aligns a frozen LRM via guidance tokens, and distills the guided behavior into model parameters.To address the lack of metrics for replacement quality beyond leakage, we further introduce Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS), which captures structural stability, fluency, and unsupported substitutes in forgotten outputs. Extensive experiments on R-TOFU and a STAR-1-derived harmful-intent setting show that GUARD substantially reduces unsafe and privacy disclosures across two widely adopted distilled LRMs while preserving reasoning utility. Codes are available at https://github.com/zeyu-Yan/GUARD

</details>


### [197] [Listen Before You Speak: Response Planning from Listener Facial Reactions for Conversational Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21683)
*Yunji Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReACT-TTS：用前一秒听者面部序列预测对话回应的情绪与韵律，两阶段框架，MELD协议下优于纯文本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话语音依赖对话上下文以及听者紧邻的前置行为，但现有方法多只依赖文本，忽略了听者即时反应这一非语言线索。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段利用一秒前置听者面部序列表征（时序条件）规划下一句话的情绪与韵律，并结合文本条件；第二阶段将预测的回应风格接入Grad-TTS骨干实现端到端语音合成。采用严格二元MELD协议，进行十种子对比、视觉变体与早晚差异消融、循环错配对照，以及20位语音研究者的语境适当性主观评测。

Result: 十种子下时序条件在平均宏F1与VAD一致性上高于纯文本，准确率基本不变；消融显示时序建模在视觉变体中表现最佳，显式早晚差分不必要；正确听者反应平均优于循环错配；主观评测中76%偏好时序、9%偏好纯文本、15%无偏好。

Conclusion: 结果支持将前置听者动态作为对话回应规划的补充线索；代码已开源。

Abstract: Conversational speech depends on dialogue context and the listener's immediately preceding behavior. We propose ReACT-TTS, a two-stage framework that uses a one-second pre-response listener facial sequence to plan the next utterance's emotion and prosody before speech realization. On a strict dyadic MELD protocol, Temporal conditioning yields higher mean macro-F1 and VAD concordance than Text-only across ten seeds, while accuracy remains essentially unchanged. Ablations show that temporal modeling performs best among the visual variants and that an explicit early-to-late difference is unnecessary; correct listener reactions also outperform cyclic mismatches on average. In a contextual-appropriateness study with 20 speech researchers, 76% of judgments prefer Temporal, 9% Text-only, and 15% report no preference. We further connect the predicted response style to a Grad-TTS backbone for end-to-end speech realization. Overall, the results support pre-response listener dynamics as complementary cues for conversational response planning. The source code is available at https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public.

</details>


### [198] [World Modeling in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21748)
*Pierre Beckmann,Matthieu Queloz,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以TaxiGPT为例，通过机制分析与因果干预，将模型的行为失败归因于叠加交叉口特征的干扰，而非缺乏世界模型，并提出机制性指标比较模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为失败常被解读为模型缺乏世界模型，即便模型已学到忠实的环境表征。作者希望纠正这种从行为表现直接推断内部表征的误解，倡导从"是否有世界模型"转向机制性研究"如何进行世界建模"。

Method: 以在曼哈顿随机游走上训练的transformer——TaxiGPT为对象，进行机制分析与因果干预。考察其对交叉口、街道的表征、位置追踪与目标罗盘导航；分析失败来源，并检验"affordance packing"（将具有相同合法移动的交叉口表征分组）的作用；提出机制性指标用于模型比较与训练阶段分析。

Result: 模型确实能表征交叉口与街道、追踪位置并使用目标罗盘导航。失败源于叠加交叉口特征之间的干扰，破坏了内部地图中的定位。Affordance packing有助于限制这些错误的后果。机制性指标显示世界建模能力在不同训练阶段出现。

Conclusion: 应从追问"模型是否拥有世界模型"转向机制性地研究其世界建模——即它表征环境并据此指导行为的相互作用能力。行为失败不必然意味着缺乏世界模型，可能只是表征干扰导致的局部错误。

Abstract: Behavioral failures can make a transformer appear to lack a world model even when it has learned faithful representations of its environment. We demonstrate this in TaxiGPT, a transformer trained on random walks through Manhattan whose failures have been interpreted as evidence of an incoherent internal map. Through mechanistic analysis and causal interventions, we show that the model represents intersections and streets, tracks its position, and uses a goal compass to navigate. We trace its failures to interference between superposed intersection features, which disrupts localization within the internal map. Affordance packing, which groups representations of intersections with the same legal moves, helps limit the consequences of these errors. Finally, we propose mechanistic indicators that we use to compare models and show that world-modeling capacities emerge at different stages of training. Our findings motivate a shift from asking whether a model has a world model to mechanistically studying its world modeling: the interacting capacities through which it represents its environment and uses those representations to guide behavior.

</details>


### [199] [ECG Mirage: Revealing and Mitigating the Underutilisation of ECGs in Vision-Language Models for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21755)
*Jinning Liang,Mingcheng Zhu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了急诊多模态预测中的"ECG Mirage"现象：视觉语言模型虽能联合处理文本与心电图，但性能提升并不代表真正利用了患者自身的ECG信息，并提出视觉提示微调作为缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊决策依赖病史、生命体征、检验和心电图（ECG）等异质信息。视觉语言模型（VLM）可联合处理这些模态，但预测性能强并不一定意味着模型真正依赖了正确患者的ECG信息。作者将此失败模式称为"ECG Mirage"，并将其区分为ECG忽视（ECG几乎无预测增益）和ECG混淆（匹配ECG优于无图输入，但不优于错配ECG）两种形式，旨在系统评估并缓解该问题。

Method: 在固定临床文本与预测目标的前提下，对比三种输入的预测结果：匹配ECG、与结局不一致的错配ECG、以及无图像输入。在MDS-ED数据集上评估四个VLM。随后保持VLM主干冻结，用监督学习加条件直接偏好优化（conditional DPO）训练四个受限视觉提示（visual prompts）。

Result: 在四个VLM上，匹配ECG对ICU入住和临床恶化预测均未带来一致优势，表明存在ECG Mirage。经视觉提示微调后，模型在ICU入住预测上达到70.6%的平衡准确率，在临床恶化预测上达67.5%，并将匹配与错配之间的性能差距分别扩大到约16.5和5.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究识别并量化了多模态临床预测中的"ECG Mirage"现象，说明仅凭预测性能无法判断模型是否真正利用了患者特异性ECG信息；视觉提示微调是一种高效且不需改动主干的缓解策略，可增强模型对正确患者ECG的依赖。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) decision-making relies on heterogeneous clinical information, including patient history, vital signs, laboratory results, and electrocardiograms (ECGs). Vision--language models (VLMs) can jointly process these modalities, but strong predictive performance does not necessarily imply meaningful use of the correct patient's ECG. We term this failure mode ECG Mirage: apparent multimodal capability without useful dependence on patient-specific ECG information. We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [200] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一个简单统计流水线（滑动窗口统计量 + MAD鲁棒打分 + 特征子集选择）在单变量时间序列异常检测上超越多数神经/统计基线，接近最强预训练基础模型；消融显示候选特征池决定性能上限，搜索策略仅为二阶因素；进一步用多模态LLM生成候选池，与手工池互补，联合选择可将VUS-PR提升至0.588。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格选择协议下，简单统计流水线能在单变量时间序列异常检测中达到何种水平，并厘清性能瓶颈究竟来自特征搜索策略还是候选特征池本身。

Method: 在滑动窗口上提取一小组统计量，用直推式鲁棒（MAD）模型为每个窗口打分，并在留出调优集上按域选择特征子集；通过三种选择策略与事后oracle、聚合网格、候选池变化进行消融；再用多模态LLM结合该域的上下文示例窗口生成候选统计池，并在生成池与手工池的并集上做选择。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U上达到0.529 per-series VUS-PR，超过公开榜单最佳神经（0.45）与统计（0.44）条目，距最强预训练基础模型仅0.06。消融中：选择策略变化仅带动0.031，聚合网格0.096，而候选池变化带动0.226。LLM生成池在匹配选择下与手工池相当且覆盖不同域；在十二组生成器-种子对中并集选择均优于生成池，使流水线提升至0.588，追平榜单最佳条目。

Conclusion: 候选特征池设定性能上限，而对其的搜索仅属二阶影响；LLM生成候选池与手工池互补，二者联合可显著提升简单统计流水线，达到与榜单最优相当的检测性能。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [201] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIST，一种以基因组特征为查询、病理组织学上下文为键值的多模态生存预测方法，缓解外部队列漂移与计算复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生存模型可整合全切片图像与基因组信息的互补预后价值，但有效融合受外部队列分布漂移和计算复杂度限制；现有方法多仅在最终阶段融合独立编码的模态，信息交互不足。

Method: MIST将基因组特征表示为token，让其在生存预测前查询由基础模型提取的紧凑病理组织学上下文token，从而在分子信息中注入组织学背景；训练目标结合离散时间生存预测、基因组特征掩码、WSI dropout以及配对的WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠、肾脏、肺和胶质母细胞瘤四个外部队列评估中，MIST在主要比较上相较标准融合基线提升了外部C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤结局预测策略，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [202] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EnterpriseVal——一个用例级的企业生成式AI评估系统，用形式化配置规范、多维指标、校准评分协议、可执行阈值门与价值风险模型，把"模型能做什么"转化为"该工作流在本企业是否值得规模化"的部署决策，并在某全球银行的三个工作流中试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型已能产出专家评审认可的专业成果，但多数企业GenAI项目未能体现可衡量的业务效果，大量智能体项目预计被取消。作者认为这本质上是测量问题：公开基准回答的是"模型能做什么"，而部署决策需要回答"该工作流在当前数据、控制条件下是否适配、可靠、安全且值得规模化"，二者之间存在缺口。

Method: 提出 EnterpriseVal 用例级评估系统，包含五部分：(i) 用例与被测"冻结社会技术配置"（模型、提示、检索、工具、护栏、人工监督）的形式化规范，并以自主性等级与后果分级共同决定评估强度；(ii) 覆盖保真度、效用、效率、可靠性、保障与监督的指标目录；(iii) 通过预测驱动推断（PPI）将盲法专家判断与校准的LLM-as-judge打分相结合的评分协议；(iv) 以可执行算法表述的两级阈值门，将带置信界的指标向量映射为 REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE 决策；(v) 将评审者捕获率作为可测参数的价值—风险模型。

Result: 在某全球银行三个工作流的试点中：信贷备忘录撰写场景下，最佳模型的人工评分引用精确度达88%、幻觉率1.6%，对应门槛分别为70%与5%；流程改造场景下，分析师单文档精修工作量从估计的27.4小时降至2.9小时。论文明确区分了已确立结果、试点证据、所提系统与开放假设。

Conclusion: 企业GenAI落地失败在很大程度上是测量与决策框架缺失所致，EnterpriseVal 通过用例级、配置冻结、多维指标与阈值门控的评估流程弥合该缺口。试点显示其在金融场景具备可行性，但系统仍属提出阶段，需通过作者指定的实验完成全面验证。

Abstract: Frontier language models now produce professional deliverables that expert graders judge to match human work on a substantial share of economically valuable tasks, yet most enterprise GenAI initiatives fail to show a measurable business effect and a large fraction of agentic projects are expected to be cancelled. We argue that this is substantially a measurement problem: public benchmarks answer "what can the model do?", whereas a deployment decision requires "is this workflow fit, reliable, safe and worth scaling - here, on our data, under our controls?". We present EnterpriseVal, a use-case-level evaluation system that closes this gap. It comprises (i) a formal specification of the use case and of the frozen socio-technical configuration under test, model, prompts, retrieval, tools, guardrails and human oversight, with an autonomy level and consequence tier that jointly set the required evaluation intensity; (ii) a metric catalogue spanning fidelity, utility, efficiency, reliability, assurance and oversight; (iii) a grading protocol that scales blinded expert judgement with calibrated LLM-as-judge scoring through prediction-powered inference; (iv) a two-tier threshold gate, stated as an executable algorithm, that maps metric vectors with confidence bounds to REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE decisions; and (v) a value-and-risk model in which the reviewer catch rate is a measured parameter. We report a pilot across three workflows in a global bank. In credit-memo drafting, human-graded citation precision reached 88% and hallucination rate 1.6% for the best model against gates of 70% and 5%; in procedure transformation, analyst refinement effort fell from an estimated 27.4 to 2.9 hours per document. We separate established results, documented pilot evidence, the proposed system and open hypotheses, and specify the experiments required for full validation

</details>


### [203] [AutoRecLab: Describe the Experiment, Get the Code!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21863)
*Moritz Baumgart,Philipp Meister,Justus Krell,Michael Schmidt,Bela Gipp,Joeran Beel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRecLab：一个基于Python的自主推荐系统实验平台，可将自然语言研究想法自动转化为可执行的RecSys实验代码，通过RAG、静态类型验证与执行引导树搜索实现从原型到完整实验的迭代构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统研究中，经验评估是核心环节，但将实验设计转化为可执行代码的过程仍然依赖人工且容易出错，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 提出AutoRecLab，一个基于Python的自主RecSys实验平台。给定自然语言提示，系统推导出明确的实验需求，构建并验证原型，然后迭代扩展为完整实验。工作流结合了用于文档查找的检索增强生成（RAG）、静态类型验证以及执行引导的树搜索。

Result: 在演示中，AutoRecLab自主实现了显式到隐式反馈转换研究。在六种算法和三个数据集的基线对比中，9次运行有8次成功，使用GPT-5.4-mini时每次运行平均成本约1美元。

Conclusion: AutoRecLab展示了利用自然语言提示自动执行RecSys实验的可行性，在成功率和成本上表现良好，有望减少人工编码负担与出错风险。

Abstract: Empirical evaluation is central to recommender-systems (RecSys) research, but turning experimental designs into executable code remains a manual and error-prone task. We present AutoRecLab, a Python-based autonomous RecSys lab that automates RecSys experiments from natural-language prompts. Given a research idea, AutoRecLab derives explicit experiment requirements, builds and validates a prototype, and iteratively expands it into the requested full experiment. The workflow combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for documentation lookup, static type verification, and execution-steered tree search. In our demonstration, AutoRecLab autonomously implements an explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion study. In a baseline comparison across six algorithms and three datasets, 8 of 9 runs succeed at an average cost of approx- imately $1 per run with GPT-5.4-mini.

</details>


### [204] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RAVEL，一个面向交互式行人重识别的检索感知在线强化学习框架，通过完整问答-检索循环的排序反馈来优化提问策略，在Interactive-PEDES上五轮交互中持续提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式检索在证据不完整时本质上是序列化信息获取问题：智能体需判断哪个问题能为下一次检索更新带来最有用的证据。现有系统通过模仿候选QA对的离线排序来训练该决策，但问题价值实际取决于其引出的回答及对检索的下游影响。论文指出候选区分度和主观有用性对该目标只能提供弱监督。

Method: 提出RAVEL框架：从监督式问题生成初始化，直接观测当前Top-4候选，并利用完整问答-检索循环的排序反馈，以在线强化学习方式优化提问策略。

Result: 在Interactive-PEDES上，RAVEL在五轮交互中取得逐步增强的检索性能。进一步分析表明，RAVEL将提问预算重新分配到局部化的开放式属性上，这类问题提供更有用的检索证据，并在初始困难查询上取得最大提升。

Conclusion: 问题价值应由其引出的回答及对检索的下游影响来定义，而非离线模仿排序；检索感知的在线强化学习能有效优化提问策略，并通过聚焦开放式局部属性显著改善困难查询的检索效果。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [205] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoViewMem 提出一种“表示优先”的数据驱动框架，将长期对话记忆在写入阶段组织为自配置、低重叠的语义视图，从而让标准 top-K 检索即可获取聚焦证据，在 LoCoMo 与 PersonaMem 基准上提升了长期问答与个性化表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体的长期记忆系统多依赖固定粒度或静态模式，当偏好、事件、约束、时间更新等异构信息混杂在同一表示中时会产生语义干扰，导致 top-K 检索对噪声敏感、相关证据排名靠后。因此需要一种能在写入阶段就完成语义解耦的记忆组织方式。

Method: AutoViewMem 从交互轨迹中发现候选语义视图，选取紧凑且互补的视图集合，并用这些视图指导写入时的结构化抽取，生成带出处（provenance-grounded）的记忆。该表示优先设计把语义解耦从检索时前移到写入时，使标准 top-K 相似度检索无需显式路由或迭代检索即可获取聚焦证据；此外还应用离线整合以提升记忆的紧凑性与一致性。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 PersonaMem 两个基准上，分别以 Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-14B 为骨干模型进行实验，AutoViewMem 在长期问答与个性化任务上均优于强记忆基线，同时保持简单的推理流程。

Conclusion: 通过将语义解耦前移到写入阶段并采用自配置、低重叠的语义视图组织记忆，AutoViewMem 在不增加检索复杂度的前提下有效缓解了异构信息混合带来的语义干扰，为 LLM 智能体的长期记忆管理提供了一种简洁而有效的方案。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [206] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了运动应激与跨会话变化下的心电身份识别鲁棒性，采用Siamese ResNet多导联晚期融合，在运动-时间感知协议下取得会话内静息对峰值EER 1.7%、CYBHi数据集3.9%的先进结果，支持存在抗生理与时间漂移的固有心脏签名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电具有个体特异模式，可用于身份识别，并有助于保护敏感心脏数据及作为自监督学习的预训练任务。但多数研究局限于单会话静息数据，对时间与生理变化的鲁棒性缺乏检验，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建Siamese ResNet，采用晚期多导联融合策略；在来自心肺运动试验的大规模心电数据集上训练，并使用运动与时间感知的评估协议，同时结合公开基准数据集进行评测。

Result: 在生理与时间双重变化条件下，实现会话内静息到峰值状态的EER为1.7%，并在CYBHi数据集上达到3.9%的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持存在一种对生理与时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏签名，表明心电生物特征在现实条件下具备可行性。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [207] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出探针方法PIR，通过读取模型内部状态识别其"认得"却未报出的正确答案，可区分"不愿答"与"不能答"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型可能隐藏已知信息（如评测中故意放水、答案与内部知识相悖），仅凭输出无法判断模型是"藏答案"还是"不知道"，这对能力评测审计与遗忘验证构成障碍，因此需要一种不依赖外部诚实参照模型或标注真值的方法来探测内部认知。

Method: 借鉴取证学中的隐藏信息测试（CIT）：向模型呈现问题及其多个候选答案，观察模型内部状态对哪个候选项反应更强，从而读出它"认出"的正确项。该方法称为PIR（Probe of Internal Recognition），无需参考模型、无需真值语料，并可扩展到自由生成。

Result: 在5个模型家族（Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi）共8个模型上，PIR以0.70–0.87的平衡准确率恢复被认出的答案，显著高于未知项基线0.28–0.40与0.25的随机水平。在提示诱导欺骗、训练得到的沙袋行为、以及外部密码锁定与电路破坏检查点等多种隐藏形式下，识别率保持0.85–0.93。当模型隐藏已知答案时识别仍高；当遗忘技术真的移除知识时，识别降到模型从未学过的问题水平。

Conclusion: PIR能区分"不愿回答"与"无法回答"的模型，支持沙袋行为审计与遗忘验证；该信号具有因果性，提供超越黑盒行为线索的信息，并可从多选题推广到自由文本生成。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [208] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CodeMidas流水线，仅用源代码将已实现功能转化为可执行强化学习环境，构建了多语言多领域的编程任务数据集，训练编码智能体并显著提升多项基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练有能力的编程智能体需要多样且带可靠验证器的任务。开源代码库是丰富来源，但现有方法依赖issue、commit等开发artifact，限制了可提取任务的范围，因而需要更好地扩展RL环境。

Method: CodeMidas是一种agentic流水线，仅以源代码作为任务特定输入，将已有代码库中的已实现功能转为可执行RL环境。它对环境构建各阶段分配智能体计算：智能体探索已实现功能以形成行为规范，基于原始代码执行构建测试，并通过执行检查和重复解快照验证与过滤候选任务。

Result: 数据集包含来自3,185个开源代码库的5,545个训练任务，覆盖23种编程语言和15个技术领域。用GRPO训练MiMo-V2.5在五个基准上均有提升：问题修复DeepSWE +11.7%、整程序构建ProgramBench +17%、终端任务Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%。消融显示增加高质量训练任务数量可提升性能；轨迹分析显示RL训练后的智能体表现出更强的代码库探索与更多样的自我验证行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明源代码可作为构建RL环境的可扩展基础，能在多样软件任务上改进编程智能体。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [209] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种持续适应框架，让冻结的前沿模型通过230多个工具操作专业设计软件，并用外部自然语言技能的程序性记忆从经验中积累和精炼可复用设计流程，在无权重更新、无人工标注的情况下显著提升设计任务执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长时程智能体任务，其结构化可编辑产物源于众多相互依赖的动作，但结果缺乏可靠的程序化评判标准（oracle）。这使传统依赖可验证反馈的适应方法难以适用，因此需要一种能在噪声大、不可验证反馈下持续适应的方案。

Method: 框架包含一个冻结的前沿模型，通过230多个工具操作专业设计软件；外部程序性记忆以自然语言技能形式积累并精炼可复用设计流程。记忆通过两种机制演化：拓宽（为反复出现的未覆盖子任务获取新流程）和加深（依据自身成功与失败执行修订现有流程）；并设有一个匹配回放门（matched replay gate），仅接纳能修复失败且不使已观察到的成功发生退化的变更。整个过程无需权重更新和人工标签。

Result: 在1,406个真实用户需求和1,869条自动评分轨迹上进行五轮实验，技能库从76个源自文档的技能增长到139个。在Claude-Sonnet-4上，GenEval2执行成功率从72.7%提升至99.3%（生成质量提升11.99分）；在四个专业设计基准上，相比无技能智能体分别取得61.8%和67.6%的胜率（Claude-Sonnet-4和Claude-Opus-4.6）。两种机制联合有效：在用户流量基准的200个留出需求上，单独拓宽或加深分别达到49.4%/48.6%的胜率，二者结合达58.5%（p = 0.025）。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆为智能体在噪声大、不可验证反馈下的持续适应提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>
